   383    *GET, FR_r250_a125(I), MODE, I, FREQ  
   384  *ENDDO  
   385      
   386  ! BUGFIX 2026-07-21: *VWRITE is a vector command -- calling it INSIDE a *DO 
   387  ! loop with array-indexed args does not print one row per iteration, it 
   388  ! dumps the WHOLE remaining array (from the current start index to NMODES)  
   389  ! on every call, producing a triangular cascade of repeated/redundant rows  
   390  ! (previously recovered by taking only the first NMODES-row block; see  
   391  ! FutureWork memory 2026-07-21). Fixed to match the _ip_pass() block below: 
   392  ! build the mode-index and Omega_lit arrays once, then *VWRITE the full 
   393  ! vectors in a SINGLE call outside any loop.
